Implementation details:
- Use FFT length L = next_power_two(2n-1) for linear convolution.
- To represent a: vector length n, zero pad to L.

- Need C nonnegative time for sqrt? Not required. But a^2 coefficients c are nonnegative; we modify within [0,cap], so c nonnegative.

Let's plan implement a function optimize_n(n, time_budget, rng, parent) that returns best score, a. It will use:
  1. Generate starts: parent (if length n), random positive, chirp, zadoff, multi_freq, spectral_flat (several alphas), maybe binary-like with dense? (no zeros? Can include zeros? We'll allow clip 0; initializers can have zeros). Also previous height_sequence_1 if size matches.
  2. For each start, run short `plateau_gradient_block` (Adam/L-BFGS) for per_start time (e.g., 3-10s), update best.
  3. Use best as seed for longer `spectral_illumination` iterations and L-BFGS polishing.
  4. Periodically do global escapes: spectral phase randomization (using FFT of a, randomize phases of A, project) and spectral flattening (c->sqrt) from current best.
  5. Final high-plateau Adam/L-BFGS.

def project_simplex_fast(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = v.sum()

Pseudo for `local_optimize(a0,n,S,L,max_time,rng, beta_init, beta_end, frac_init, frac_end, use_lbfgs=True)`:
```

C,A=conv; cmax best

  weights = weights_from_C(C,beta,frac); if frac maybe include beta? We'll use combined: if beta>0: w_lse; if frac>0: w_plate; w=0.5*(w_lse+w_plate) if both else.
  g = 2*ifft(fft(w)*conj(A))[:n]
  # Sum-zero correction? g = g - g.mean()
  if use_lbfgs:
     d = lbfgs_dir(...)
  else: d = -g
  # normalize direction to unit norm
  nd = norm(d); if nd small break
  d = d/nd
  alpha=cur_alpha
  best_c=cmax; best_a=a; best_g? 
  for ls in range(8):
    cand = safe_project(a + alpha*d, S)
    Cc,Ac=conv(cand); cc=Cc.max()
    # optional surrogate: if beta>0, val=cmax+logsumexp/beta; accept if val < val old? We can compute old val when weights? harder.
    if cc < best_c - 1e-12: best_c=cc; best_a=cand
    if cc <= cmax: break
    alpha *=0.5
  if best_c < cmax - 1e-12:
     a=best_a; C=best_C; A=best_A; g_new=grad from new
     update best global
     sy update history if success
     cur_alpha=min(cur_alpha*1.1, max_alpha)
     stall=0
  else:

     cur_alpha*=0.6
     stall+=1

  if it%50: beta=min(beta_end,beta*1.05); frac=max(0.001, frac*0.98)
  if it%200: exact projection? not.
```

def search_n(n,budget,rng,parent):

 # Stage 0: initial candidates list with times short

 inits.append(('flat_blend', ... from rand?))
 inits.append(('rand', make_init_rand))
 inits.append(('chirp', ...))
 inits.append(('zadoff_abs', ...))
 inits.append(('fourier_sign', make_init_fourier_flat?))
 inits.append(('deflation', deflation_init? maybe too slow; skip or short))
 # Run local_optimize on each for per_init time 5-20s
 for init: local for 300s total?
 # Stage 1: best as starting, longer local + spectral flatten alternation.
 while time:
   block_time=min(120, remaining)
   # with probability run spectral_flat_block for 30s, then local
   a,c=spectral_flat_block(best, 120s)
   update
   a,c=local_optimize(a, remaining)
   update
   if stuck: spectral_phase_restart with random frac, then local

 return best
```

Need implement `sqrt_F_signed(F, rng)`:
```
def sqrt_freq_signed(F, rng, randomize=True):
    L=len(F); A=np.empty(L, dtype=complex); half=L//2
    A[0]=np.sqrt(F[0])  # F[0] should real positive
    if half>0:
       # Nyquist
       val=np.real(F[half])
       if val<0: val=abs(val)
       A[half]=np.sqrt(val)
       if half>1:
          idx=np.arange(1,half)
          sk=np.sqrt(F[idx])
          if randomize:
             # random signs for about half frequencies? All signs would destroy F; but we choose ±. Use mostly + with some random?
             sgn = np.where(rng.random(half-1) < 0.2, -1.0, 1.0)
          else: sgn=np.ones(half-1)
          A[idx]=sk*sgn
          A[L-idx]=np.conj(A[idx])
    return A
```
Randomizing many signs may make a_raw closer to positive? Maybe. To maintain A^2 approx F, signs matter only phase π; A^2 unaffected by sign (since (-s)^2=+s^2) for each paired frequency? Wait if A[L-k]=conj(A[k]) and sgn real, A^2 at k = (sgn^2)F[k]=F[k], so signs do not change A^2! Thus square of A remains F regardless of sign flips (with paired signs real ±). Great. Different sign patterns yield different a (polynomials) all with same square C! This is useful. We can search signs to maximize nonnegative/front segment. Since signs don't change C, the cyclic convolution length L of a (full length) is fixed C. But we select a segment length n and clip; the linear conv of clipped/truncated segment differs. Choosing sign pattern can make the segment more nonnegative and linear conv closer to C. Excellent.

```
def sqrt_target_search(Ctarget, L, n, S, max_time, rng, max_iters_per_phase=None, n_offset_candidates=13):
    F = fft(Ctarget, L)
    base = sqrt_freq_signed(F, rng, randomize=False)  # principal
    # sign indices: 1..L//2 (including Nyquist?) Let's include 1..half (half=L//2). For k=half, base value real maybe sign can be +/- (imag sign dropped). Include.
    sign_indices = np.arange(1, half+1)  # half if even
    signs = np.ones(len(sign_indices))
    A = base.copy()
    best = None; best_score=inf
    t0=time.monotonic()
    its=0
    # initial offsets maybe determined.
    while time < max_time:
       if its % 50: maybe randomize 5% signs
       # random flip count
       nflip = rng.integers(1, max(2, half//100)+1)  # 1..~80
       for pos in rng.choice(len(sign_indices), nflip, replace=False): signs[pos]*=-1; k=sign_indices[pos]; A[k]*=-1; A[L-k]*=-1 if k!=half else A[k]*=-1
       # evaluate current A
       a_raw = real(ifft(A))
       # find candidate offset using pos sum running
       pos = np.maximum(a_raw, 0.0)
       # circular valid sums length n: use np.convolve? n maybe 8192, pos length L; convolution length L-n+1 O(Ln) too heavy. Use integral image with periodic:
       # prefix of doubled pos.
       # Simpler try offset 0 and maybe where a_raw has max cumulative? We can do coarse grid: offsets = 0, L//4, L//2, 3L//4 plus best around max of positive mass.
       # Running sum can be computed O(L):
       pos2 = np.concatenate([pos, pos[:n]])
       cum = np.concatenate([[0], np.cumsum(pos2)])
       sums = cum[n:n+L] - cum[:L]
       r0 = int(np.argmax(sums))
       # evaluate r0 plus neighbors maybe 5
       best_a=None; best_c=inf
       for r in neighbors(r0, L, 5):
          seg = a_raw[r:r+n]; if wrap use concat or np.roll? Once per eval use np.roll(a_raw, -r)[:n] (alloc). For 11 offsets, costly. Use if r+n<=L: seg=a_raw[r:r+n] else seg=np.concatenate(...)
```

def convolve_a(a, L, n):
    A = fft(a, n=L)
    C = real(ifft(A*A, n=L))[:2*n-1]
    C = np.where(C < 0, 0.0, C)
    return C, A
```
Potential: `ifft(A*A)` where A from real a, A*A not conjugate, can have imaginary? Should be real if A is DFT of real? A^2 DFT of linear conv (real), so real. Good.
3. `weights_lse(C, beta)`:
```
def weights_lse(C, beta):
    m=C.size; cmax=C.max()
    if beta < 1e-6: return np.full(m,1/m)
    e=np.exp(beta*(C-cmax))
    s=e.sum()
    if s <=0: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
    return e/s
```
4. `weights_top(C, frac)`:
```
if frac <=0: return None
thr=cmax-frac*(cmax+1e-12)
w=np.maximum(C-thr,0)
s=w.sum(); if s<...: w=np.ones_like(C)
return w/s
```
5. `combined_weights(C,beta,frac)`:
```
wl=lse if beta>0 else None; wt=top if frac>0 else None
if both: w=0.5*(wl+wt)
elif ...
normalize
```

       W=combined...
       g=grad...

       d = d - d.mean()

       alpha=cur_alpha
       best_cand=None; best_cand_c=cmax

Spectral phase restart top-level:
```
def spectral_phase_restart(a,L,n,S, rng, frac=0.5):
    A=fft(a,L)
    mag=np.abs(A); phase=np.angle(A)
    if frac>=1: newphase=rng.uniform(0,2π,L)
    else: newphase=phase.copy(); idx=rng.choice(L, int(L*frac)); newphase[idx]=rng.uniform...
    a_new=real(ifft(mag*exp(1j newphase)))[:n]
    return safe_nat(a_new,S)
```

   sums = cum[n:n+L] - np.concatenate(([0.0], cum[:L-1]))? Need length L vector: valid windows starting r=0..L-1 length n.

`sqrt_target_search` detailed implementation:
```
def sqrt_target_search(Ctarget, L, n, S, max_time, rng, n_offsets=3, verbose=True):
    # ensure Ctarget shape L, nonnegative, sum S2
    F = fft(Ctarget, n=L)
    half = L//2
    base = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.complex128)
    f0 = F[0]
    if abs(f0.imag)>1e-12: f0 = complex(float(f0.real),0.0)
    base[0] = np.sqrt(f0)
    # Nyquist
    base[half] = np.sqrt(F[half])  # may be complex
    if half > 1:
        idx = np.arange(1, half)
        sk = np.sqrt(F[idx])
        base[idx] = sk
        base[L-idx] = np.conj(sk)
    # If F[0] small, base ok
    sign_idx = np.arange(1, half+1)
    A = base.copy()
    best_A = A.copy()
    best_a = None; best_c = np.inf; best_score = np.inf
    start=time.monotonic(); stall=0; iters=0; no_imp=0
    # helper internal not allowed? We can't define nested function. Need top-level functions. Evaluation code duplicated in loop.
```
Cannot nest helper. We'll write evaluation inline. But need a function to build candidate from A to avoid duplication? Top-level `evaluate_sign_candidate(A,L,n,S,offset_candidates)` can be top-level. It will compute a_raw, offsets, score. Good.

`evaluate_sign_candidate(A, L, n, S, rng=None)`: uses `np.maximum(a_raw,0)`, prefix, offsets [0,1,-1]. Deterministic. No random.
```
def evaluate_sign_candidate(A, L, n, S):
    a_raw = np.real(ifft(A, n=L))
    pos = np.maximum(a_raw, 0.0)
    p2 = np.concatenate((pos, pos[:n]))
    cum = np.empty(n+L+1)? Use np.cumsum
    # memory: pos length L, concat length L+n ~2n, okay.
    sums = cum[n:n+L] - cum[:L]
    r = int(np.argmax(sums))
    best_a=None; best_c=inf
    for dr in (0,1,-1):
       r2=(r+dr)%L
       if r2+n <= L: seg=a_raw[r2:r2+n]
       else: seg=np.concatenate((a_raw[r2:L], a_raw[:r2+n-L]))
       segc=np.maximum(seg,0.0)
       ssum=segc.sum()
       if ssum < 1e-12: continue
       a=segc*(S/ssum)
       if a.sum() < 0.01? no
       Cc=convolve_a(a,L,n)[0]
       cc=Cc.max()
       if cc<best_c: best_c=cc; best_a=a.copy()
    return best_a,best_c
```
This calls convolve_a which computes FFT and ifft. It also computes A? The A of candidate not returned. Good.

Target C generation top-level `make_target_C_from_a(a_best, L,n,S,theta, cap_extra=1.02)`:
```
def make_target_C(a, L,n,S, theta, cap_factor=1.1):
    Ccur,_=convolve_a(a,L,n)
    Umean=S*S/float(2*n-1)
    U=np.zeros(L); U[:2*n-1]=Umean
    cap=cap_factor*max(Ccur.max(), Umean)
    Cc=np.minimum(Ccur, cap)
    Ct=(1-theta)*Cc + theta*U
    Ct=np.maximum(Ct,0)
    ssum=Ct.sum()
    if ssum>0: Ct*=S*S/ssum
    return Ct
```
If `a` None, return U.

def make_init_chirp(n,rng): t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False); a=1+0.6*np.sin(2π(3t+0.7t^2))+0.2*rng.normal; clip min1e-6
def make_init_zadoff(n,rng): c=rng.integers(1,n); i=arange; phase=-π i(2i+1-c)/n; a=1+0.7*np.cos(phase)+0.1*normal; clip min1e-6

`convolve_a` top-level:
```
def convolve_a(a, L, n):
    A = fft(a, n=L)
    C = np.real(ifft(A * A, n=L))[:2*n-1]
    C = np.where(C < 0.0, 0.0, C)
    return C, A
```
Potential: `A*A` might overflow? a~0.015, A~S=128, A^2~16384, fine.

`evaluate_sign_candidate`:
Need global `convolve_a`. It will call `convolve_a` for up to 3 candidates. To reduce if `segc.sum()` very small. It can also avoid conv if `np.max(segc)*?` no.
```
def evaluate_sign_candidate(A, L, n, S):
    a_raw = np.real(ifft(A, n=L))
    pos = np.maximum(a_raw, 0.0)
    p2 = np.concatenate((pos, pos[:n]))
    cum = np.empty(p2.size+1, dtype=float); cum[0]=0.0; cum[1:]=np.cumsum(p2)
    sums = cum[n:n+L] - cum[:L]
    r = int(np.argmax(sums))
    best_a=None; best_c=np.inf
    for dr in (0,1,-1,2,-2? maybe 5 costs): only 3 for speed.
       rr = (r+dr) % L
       if rr + n <= L:
          seg = a_raw[rr:rr+n]
       else:
          seg = np.concatenate((a_raw[rr:L], a_raw[:rr+n-L]))
       seg = np.maximum(seg, 0.0)
       ss = seg.sum()
       if ss < 0.02 * S: continue  # maybe too high? Use 1e-12; if positive mass very low, after scaling high values maybe but could still good? If mean positive low, amplitude high -> high c. But not invalid. Use ss>1e-12.
       a = seg * (S / ss)
       Cc = convolve_a(a, L, n)[0]
       cc = float(Cc.max())
       if cc < best_c:
          best_c=cc; best_a=a.copy()
    return best_a,best_c
```
If best_a None, return None, inf. The `ss<1e-12` only.

def sqrt_target_search(Ctarget, L, n, S, max_time, rng, label="sx"):
    # ensure Ctarget copy
    Ct = np.maximum(np.asarray(Ctarget, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    if Ct.size < L: pad
    ssum=Ct.sum()
    if ssum >0: Ct *= (S*S)/ssum
    else uniform
    F=fft(Ct,n=L)
    half=L//2
    base = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.complex128)
    base[0] = np.sqrt(complex(float(np.real(F[0])),0.0)) if F[0].real>0 else 0
    base[half] = np.sqrt(F[half]) if half < L else 0
    if half > 1:
       idx = np.arange(1, half)
       sk = np.sqrt(F[idx])
       base[idx]=sk
       base[L-idx]=np.conj(sk)
    sign_idx=np.arange(1, half+1)
    A=base.copy(); best_A=A.copy()
    best_a=None; best_c=np.inf
    t_end=time.monotonic()+max(1.0,float(max_time))
    stall=0; iters=0; last_print=0
    # initial evaluate
    a_i, c_i = evaluate_sign_candidate(A,L,n,S)
    if a_i is not None and c_i<best_c: best_a=a_i; best_c=c_i; best_A=A.copy()
    # Also try all + but random signs initial? We can set A=base with random 20% signs and evaluate?
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       ...
    return best_a,best_c
```

`local_lse_block` top-level. Let's implement carefully.
```
def local_lse_block(a_in, n, S, L, max_time, rng, beta_start=20.0, beta_end=500.0, frac_start=0.1, frac_end=0.003):
    a=safe_nat(a_in,S); C,A=convolve_a(a,L,n); cmax=float(C.max()); best_c=cmax; best_a=a.copy()
    beta=float(beta_start); frac=float(frac_start)
    s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]
    base_alpha = 0.05 * math.sqrt(2.0)  # since S/√n
    max_alpha = 0.7 * math.sqrt(2.0)
    alpha=base_alpha; stall=0; it=0
    t_end=time.monotonic()+max(1.0,max_time)
    # compute initial gradient
    W=combined_weights(C,beta,frac); g=grad_from_A(A,W,L,n)
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       it+=1
       if it % 200 ==0:
          beta=min(beta_end,beta*1.05)
          frac=max(frac_end, frac*0.97)
       # Regrad if C changed? We have C/A/g current. At loop start or after a update.
       # If stall high, choose escape
       if stall > 80:
          a=spectral_phase_restart(a,L,n,S,rng, frac=0.7)
          C,A=convolve_a(a,L,n); cmax=float(C.max())
          if cmax<best_c: best_c=cmax; best_a=a.copy()
          W=combined_weights(C,beta,frac); g=grad_from_A(A,W,L,n)
          s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]; stall=0; alpha=base_alpha
          continue
       W=combined_weights(C,beta,frac)
       g=grad_from_A(A,W,L,n)
       # direction
       if len(s_hist)>0:
          d=lbfgs_direction(s_hist,y_hist,g)
       else:
          d=-g
       d = d - d.mean()
       nd=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if nd < 1e-14:
          d=-g; nd=np.linalg.norm(d)
          if nd<1e-14: break
       d = d/nd
       trial_best_c=cmax; trial_a=None; trial_C=None; trial_A=None; trial_g=None
       al=alpha
       for ls in range(7):
          cand=safe_nat(a + al*d, S)
          Cc,Ac=convolve_a(cand,L,n); cc=float(Cc.max())
          if cc < trial_best_c - 1e-12:
             trial_best_c=cc; trial_a=cand.copy(); trial_C=Cc; trial_A=Ac
             # compute grad for candidate? We can compute after selection to save.
          if cc <= cmax:
             break
          al *= 0.5
       if trial_a is not None and trial_best_c < cmax - 1e-12:
          # compute grad at trial
          Wt=combined_weights(trial_C,beta,frac); gt=grad_from_A(trial_A,Wt,L,n)
          s_k=trial_a - a; y_k=gt - g
          sy=float(np.dot(s_k,y_k)); ss=float(np.dot(s_k,s_k))
          if ss > 1e-20 and sy > 1e-14*ss:
             s_hist.append(s_k); y_hist.append(y_k)
             if len(s_hist)>10: s_hist.pop(0); y_hist.pop(0)
          a=trial_a; C=trial_C; A=trial_A; cmax=trial_best_c; g=gt
          alpha=min(max_alpha, alpha*1.12)
          stall=0
          if cmax < best_c:
             best_c=cmax; best_a=a.copy()
             if best_c < 1.30? no
             print? inside block too much. Could print every improvement if large.
       else:
          # no improvement: try smaller step, stall
          alpha=max(base_alpha*0.2, alpha*0.6)
          stall +=1
       if it % 2000 ==0 and best_c < np.inf:
          print
    return best_a, best_c
```

`combined_weights` top-level:
```
def combined_weights(C,beta,frac):
   w1=None; w2=None
   if beta>1e-6: w1=weights_lse(C,beta)
   if frac>0: w2=weights_top(C,frac)
   if w1 is not None and w2 is not None: w=0.5*(w1+w2)
   elif w1 is not None: w=w1
   elif w2 is not None: w=w2
   else: w=np.ones_like(C)/C.size
   s=w.sum(); if s<=0: w=np.ones_like(C)/C.size; return w/s
```

`grad_from_A` top-level:
```
def grad_from_A(A,W,L,n):
    Wl=fft(W, n=L)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(Wl * np.conj(A), n=L))[:n]
    return g
```
Potential: W length 2n-1, zero pad to L. Good.

`spectral_phase_restart` top-level:
```
def spectral_phase_restart(a,L,n,S,rng,frac=0.5):
    A=fft(a,n=L)
    mag=np.abs(A)
    if frac >=1: phase=rng.uniform(0,2*pi,L)
    else:
       phase=np.angle(A)
       m=int(L*frac)
       idx=rng.choice(L, size=m, replace=False)
       phase[idx]=rng.uniform(0,2*pi,m)
    a_new=np.real(ifft(mag*np.exp(1j*phase), n=L))[:n]
    return safe_nat(a_new,S)
```

def search_n(n,budget,rng,parent=None):
   S=math.sqrt(2.0*n); L=1<<(2*n-1).bit_length()
   best_a=None; best_c=inf
   deadline = time.monotonic()+max(0.0,budget-30)
   def not allowed nested. We'll do inline update.
   U=make_uniform_C(L,n,S)
   # 1 flat search time min(250, budget*0.2)
   t0=time.monotonic()
   if budget>60:
      ta=min(250.0, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.3, 300.0)
      a_f,c_f=sqrt_target_search(U,L,n,S,ta,rng,1.0,"flat")
      if a_f is not None: update
   # 2 local on flat if exists and time
   if a_f is not None:
      block_time = min(250, remaining*0.25)
      a_l,c_l=local_lse_block(a_f,... beta 5-100)
      update
   # 3 diverse inits short local
   inits=[] ...
   for each: use min(40, remaining/ninits) with local beta moderate.
   # 4 main cycles target search/local
   cycles=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
      remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
      if remaining < 60: break
      theta = min(0.9, 0.55 + 0.1*cycles)
      cap_factor = 1.05 + 0.05*min(cycles,5)
      Ct=make_target_C(best_a if best_a is not None else U?, L,n,S, theta, cap_factor)
      ts = min(180.0, remaining*0.45)
      tl = remaining - ts - 20
      if ts>20:
         a_s,c_s=sqrt_target_search(Ct,L,n,S,ts,rng,...)
         update
      if remaining-time > 30:
         a_l,c_l=local_lse_block(best_a or a_s, n,S,L, min(tl, 200), rng, beta_start=20+10*cycles, beta_end=800, frac_start=0.05, frac_end=0.001)
         update
      cycles+=1
   return best_a,best_c

`safe_nat` implementation:
```
def safe_nat(v,S):
   v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
   v=np.clip(v,0.0,1000.0)
   s=v.sum()
   if s <= 1e-12:
       return np.ones(v.size)*(S/v.size) if v.size>0 else np.zeros(0)
   out=v*(S/s)
   if out.max()>1000.0:
       out=np.clip(out,0.0,1000.0); s2=out.sum(); if s2<=... uniform; else out*=S/s2
   return out
```

Pseudo `local_lse_block` final:
```
def local_lse_block(a_in, n, S, L, max_time, rng, beta_start=10.0, beta_end=300.0, frac_start=0.15, frac_end=0.003):
    if a_in is None: a = np.full(n, S/n)
    else: a=safe_nat(a_in,S)
    C,A=convolve_a(a,L,n)
    cmax=float(C.max()); best_c=cmax; best_a=a.copy()
    beta=... frac=...
    base_alpha=0.05*math.sqrt(2.0); max_alpha=0.7*math.sqrt(2.0); alpha=base_alpha
    s_hist=[];y_hist=[];stall=0;it=0
    t_end=time.monotonic()+max(1.0,float(max_time))
    W=combined_weights(C,beta,frac); g=grad_from_A(A,W,L,n)
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       it +=1
       if it % 200 ==0:
          beta=min(beta_end,beta*1.05); frac=max(frac_end, frac*0.97)
       if stall > 100:
          a=spectral_phase_restart(a,L,n,S,rng, frac=0.7)
          C,A=convolve_a(a,L,n); cmax=float(C.max())
          if cmax < best_c: best_c=cmax; best_a=a.copy()
          W=combined_weights(C,beta,frac); g=grad_from_A(A,W,L,n)
          s_hist=[]; y_hist=[]; stall=0; alpha=base_alpha
          continue
       W=combined_weights(C,beta,frac); g=grad_from_A(A,W,L,n)
       if len(s_hist)>0:
          d=lbfgs_direction(s_hist,y_hist,g)
       else: d=-g
       d = d - d.mean()
       nd=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
       if nd < 1e-14:
          d=-g; nd=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
          if nd < 1e-14: break
       d = d/nd
       trial_best_c=cmax; trial_a=None; trial_C=None; trial_A=None
       al=alpha
       for _ in range(7):
          cand=safe_nat(a + al*d, S)
          Cc,Ac=convolve_a(cand,L,n); cc=float(Cc.max())
          if cc < trial_best_c - 1e-12:
             trial_best_c=cc; trial_a=cand.copy(); trial_C=Cc; trial_A=Ac
          if cc <= cmax:
             break
          al *= 0.5
       if trial_a is not None and trial_best_c < cmax - 1e-12:
          Wt=combined_weights(trial_C,beta,frac); gt=grad_from_A(trial_A,Wt,L,n)
          s_k=trial_a-a; y_k=gt-g
          sy=float(np.dot(s_k,y_k)); ss=float(np.dot(s_k,s_k))
          if ss > 1e-20 and sy > 1e-14*ss:
             s_hist.append(s_k); y_hist.append(y_k)
             if len(s_hist)>10: s_hist.pop(0); y_hist.pop(0)
          a=trial_a; C=trial_C; A=trial_A; cmax=trial_best_c; g=gt
          alpha = min(max_alpha, alpha*1.12)
          stall=0
          if cmax < best_c:
             best_c=cmax; best_a=a.copy()
       else:
          alpha = max(base_alpha*0.25, alpha*0.65)
          stall +=1
       if it % 2000 ==0:
          print("local n=%d cmax=%.6f stall=%d"% (n,best_c,stall))
    return best_a,best_c
```

def search_n(n, budget, rng, parent=None):
    if budget <=0: return None, inf
    S=math.sqrt(2.0*n); L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    best_a=None; best_c=np.inf
    t_start=time.monotonic(); deadline=t_start+max(0.0, float(budget)-40.0)
    U=make_uniform_C(L,n,S)
    # flat search
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 30:
       ta = min(300.0, max(0.0, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.35))
       a_f,c_f=sqrt_target_search(U,L,n,S,ta,rng)
       if a_f is not None and c_f < best_c: best_a=a_f; best_c=c_f
    # local on current best
    if best_a is None:
       best_a = np.full(n, S/n); best_c=2.0? compute conv? for state
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 30:
       tl = min(240.0, max(0.0, (deadline-time.monotonic())*0.22))
       a_l,c_l=local_lse_block(best_a,n,S,L,tl,rng, beta_start=5.0,beta_end=80.0, frac_start=0.2, frac_end=0.01)
       if a_l is not None and c_l < best_c: best_a=a_l; best_c=c_l
    # inits
    inits=[]
    if parent is not None and len(parent)==n:
       p=np.clip(parent,0.0,1000.0); s=p.sum(); if s>1e-9: inits.append(p*(S/s)); else uniform
    inits.append(make_init_rand(n,rng)*(S/...))
    inits.append(make_init_chirp(n,rng)...)
    inits.append(make_init_zadoff(n,rng)...)
    nin=len(inits)
    for arr in inits:
       if deadline - now < 60: break
       per = min(80.0, max(5.0, (deadline-now)*0.03?))
       a_l,c_l=local(...)
       if c_l<best: update

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2 .

The implementation combines:
1. A sign-space search over square roots of a target convolution C.
   For a chosen target C with sum(a)^2 norm, all sign choices of
   IFFT(sqrt(FFT(C))) square to C cyclically; we hill-climb/anneal
   signs to make a length-n nonnegative window have low actual
   linear self-convolution peak.
2. Target-C construction from the current best sequence, blended
   toward a flat target and peak-capped.
3. L-BFGS-style active-set / log-sum-exp subgradient polishing with
   simplex projection, adaptive smoothing, and spectral-phase restarts.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft


def safe_nat(v, S):
    """Clamp to nonnegative and rescale to sum S."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(v.shape, S / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    out = v * (S / s)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(out.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(v.shape, S / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return out * (S / s2)


def convolve_a(a, L, n):
    """Linear self-convolution via zero-padded FFT. Returns (C, A)."""
    A = fft(a, n=L)
    C = np.real(ifft(A * A, n=L))[:2 * n - 1]
    C = np.where(C < 0.0, 0.0, C)
    return C, A


def weights_lse(C, beta):
    """Log-sum-exp weights on convolution coefficients."""
    cmax = float(C.max())
    m = C.size
    if beta < 1e-8:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    z = beta * (C - cmax)
    z = np.where(z > -745.0, z, -745.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(C))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def weights_top(C, frac):
    """Weights on near-plateau coefficients."""
    cmax = float(C.max())
    cmin = float(C.min())
    span = cmax - cmin
    m = C.size
    if span <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    thr = cmax - max(span, 1e-12) * frac
    w = np.maximum(C - thr, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def combined_weights(C, beta, frac):
    """Blend LSE and plateau weights."""
    w1 = None
    w2 = None
    if beta > 1e-8:
        w1 = weights_lse(C, beta)
    if frac > 1e-8:
        w2 = weights_top(C, min(2.0, frac))
    if w1 is not None and w2 is not None:
        w = 0.5 * (w1 + w2)
    elif w1 is not None:
        w = w1
    elif w2 is not None:
        w = w2
    else:
        w = np.ones(C.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(C.size)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(C.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(C.size)
    else:
        w = w / s
    return w


def grad_from_A(A, W, L, n):
    """Gradient of weighted sum of c_k = conv(a,a)_k."""
    Wl = fft(W, n=L)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(Wl * np.conj(A), n=L))[:n]
    return g


def lbfgs_direction(s_hist, y_hist, g):
    """Two-loop L-BFGS direction (unnormalized)."""
    m = len(s_hist)
    gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
    if m == 0 or gn < 1e-18:
        if gn < 1e-18:
            return np.zeros_like(g)
        return -g

    q = g.copy()
    alphas = np.zeros(m)
    for i in range(m - 1, -1, -1):
        s_i = s_hist[i]
        y_i = y_hist[i]
        yy = float(np.dot(y_i, y_i))
        if yy < 1e-30:
            alphas[i] = 0.0
        else:
            alphas[i] = float(np.dot(s_i, q)) / yy
            q = q - alphas[i] * y_i

    s_last = s_hist[-1]
    y_last = y_hist[-1]
    yy_last = float(np.dot(y_last, y_last))
    if yy_last > 1e-30:
        gamma = max(1e-12, float(np.dot(s_last, y_last)) / yy_last)
    else:
        gamma = 1.0
    r = gamma * q

    for i in range(m):
        s_i = s_hist[i]
        y_i = y_hist[i]
        yy = float(np.dot(y_i, y_y_fallback := y_i, y_i)) if False else float(np.dot(y_i, y_i))
        if yy < 1e-30:
            continue
        beta_i = float(np.dot(s_i, y_i)) / yy
        r = r + (alphas[i] - beta_i) * y_i

    d = -r
    if float(np.dot(g, d)) > 0.0:
        d = -g
    return d


def spectral_phase_restart(a, L, n, S, rng, frac=0.5):
    """Randomize part of the FFT phase and project back to nonnegatives."""
    A = fft(a, n=L)
    mag = np.abs(A)
    if frac >= 1.0:
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, L)
    else:
        phase = np.angle(A)
        k = int(max(1, min(L, L * frac)))
        idx = rng.choice(L, size=k, replace=False)
        phase[idx] = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, k)
    a_new = np.real(ifft(mag * np.exp(1j * phase), n=L))[:n]
    return safe_nat(a_new, S)


def apply_sign_flips(A, L, sign_idx, half, pos):
    """Flip conjugate-pair square-root signs in-place."""
    for p in pos:
        k = int(sign_idx[p])
        A[k] *= -1.0
        if k != half:
            A[L - k] *= -1.0


def evaluate_sign_candidate(A, L, n, S):
    """Evaluate one square-root sign pattern as a length-n nonnegative sequence."""
    a_raw = np.real(ifft(A, n=L))
    pos = np.maximum(a_raw, 0.0)
    p2 = np.concatenate((pos, pos[:n]))
    cum = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(p2)))
    sums = cum[n:n + L] - cum[:L]
    r0 = int(np.argmax(sums))

    best_a = None
    best_c = np.inf

    for dr in (0, 1, -1):
        rr = (r0 + dr) % L
        if rr + n <= L:
            seg = a_raw[rr:rr + n]
        else:
            seg = np.concatenate((a_raw[rr:L], a_raw[:rr + n - L]))
        seg = np.maximum(seg, 0.0)
        ss = float(seg.sum())
        if ss < 1e-12:
            continue
        a = seg * (S / ss)
        Cc = convolve_a(a, L, n)[0]
        cc = float(Cc.max())
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_c


def make_uniform_C(L, n, S):
    """Uniform causal target convolution with sum S^2."""
    C = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    C[:2 * n - 1] = (S * S) / float(2 * n - 1)
    return C


def make_target_C(a, L, n, S, theta, cap_factor):
    """Build a target C by blending capped current convolution with uniform."""
    theta = float(np.clip(theta, 0.0, 1.0))
    cap_factor = max(1.0, float(cap_factor))
    U = make_uniform_C(L, n, S)
    if a is None:
        return U
    Ccur, _ = convolve_a(a, L, n)
    Umean = (S * S) / float(2 * n - 1)
    cap = cap_factor * Umean
    Cc = np.minimum(Ccur, cap)
    Ct = (1.0 - theta) * Cc + theta * U
    Ct = np.maximum(Ct, 0.0)
    s = float(Ct.sum())
    if s > 1e-15:
        Ct *= (S * S) / s
    else:
        Ct = U.copy()
    return Ct


def sqrt_target_search(Ctarget, L, n, S, max_time, rng):
    """Sign-space search over square roots of target C."""
    Ct = np.asarray(Ctarget, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if Ct.size != L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(L, Ct.size)
        tmp[:m] = Ct[:m]
        Ct = tmp
    Ct = np.maximum(Ct, 0.0)
    s = float(Ct.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        Ct = make_uniform_C(L, n, S)
    else:
        Ct *= (S * S) / s

    F = fft(Ct, n=L)
    half = L // 2
    base = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.complex128)

    f0 = float(np.real(F[0]))
    base[0] = np.sqrt(max(0.0, f0))
    base[half] = np.sqrt(F[half])
    if half > 1:
        idx = np.arange(1, half)
        sk = np.sqrt(F[idx])
        base[idx] = sk
        base[L - idx] = np.conj(sk)

    sign_idx = np.arange(1, half + 1)
    A = base.copy()
    best_A = A.copy()
    best_a = None
    best_c = np.inf

    # Try a few initial sign basins.
    for trial in range(12):
        if trial > 0:
            nf = max(1, min(half, half // 8))
            pos = rng.choice(half, size=nf, replace=False)
            apply_sign_flips(A, L, sign_idx, half, pos)
        a_i, c_i = evaluate_sign_candidate(A, L, n, S)
        if a_i is not None and c_i < best_c:
            best_a = a_i.copy()
            best_c = c_i
            best_A = A.copy()

    if best_a is None:
        a_i, c_i = evaluate_sign_candidate(base, L, n, S)
        if a_i is not None:
            best_a = a_i.copy()
            best_c = c_i
            best_A = base.copy()
        else:
            return None, np.inf

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + max(1.0, float(max_time))
    A = best_A.copy()
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        it += 1
        tf = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - t0) / max(1.0, float(max_time)))
        max_flip = int((1.05 - tf) * 0.18 * half) + 2
        max_flip = min(half, max(4, max_flip))
        min_flip = 1 if tf > 0.55 else max(1, max_flip // 100)
        nflip = int(rng.integers(min_flip, max_flip + 1))
        nflip = min(half, max(1, nflip))

        pos = rng.choice(half, size=nflip, replace=False)
        apply_sign_flips(A, L, sign_idx, half, pos)

        a_c, c_c = evaluate_sign_candidate(A, L, n, S)
        accepted = False
        if a_c is not None:
            if c_c < best_c * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                best_a = a_c.copy()
                best_c = c_c
                best_A = A.copy()
                stall = 0
                accepted = True
            elif c_c < best_c * 1.03:
                # Mild simulated-annealling acceptance.
                p = 0.03 + 0.18 * (1.0 - tf)
                if c_c < best_c and rng.random() < p:
                    stall = 0
                    accepted = True
                elif c_c < best_c * 1.01 and rng.random() < 0.04:
                    stall = 0
                    accepted = True

        if not accepted:
            apply_sign_flips(A, L, sign_idx, half, pos)  # revert
            stall += 1
            if stall > 1000:
                A = best_A.copy()
                nf = max(1, min(half, half // 12))
                pos2 = rng.choice(half, size=nf, replace=False)
                apply_sign_flips(A, L, sign_idx, half, pos2)
                stall = 0

        if it % 2000 == 0:
            print("sqrt_target cmax=%.6f  it=%d  stall=%d" % (best_c, it, stall))

    return best_a, best_c


def make_init_rand(n, rng):
    return 0.5 + 0.8 * rng.random(n)


def make_init_chirp(n, rng):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    a = (1.0
         + 0.65 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (2.0 * t + 0.55 * t * t) + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
         + 0.25 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (7.0 * t + 0.2 * t * t) + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
         + 0.12 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    return np.maximum(a, 1e-6)


def make_init_zadoff(n, rng):
    m = max(2, n)
    i = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    c = int(rng.integers(1, m))
    phase = -math.pi * i * (2.0 * i + 1.0 - c) / float(m)
    a = 1.0 + 0.65 * np.cos(phase) + 0.15 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return np.maximum(a, 1e-6)


def local_lse_block(a_in, n, S, L, max_time, rng,
                    beta_start=10.0, beta_end=300.0,
                    frac_start=0.15, frac_end=0.003):
    """L-BFGS-style active-set / LSE polishing."""
    if a_in is None:
        a = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = safe_nat(a_in, S)

    C, A = convolve_a(a, L, n)
    cmax = float(C.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_c = cmax

    beta = float(beta_start)
    frac = float(frac_start)
    base_alpha = 0.05 * math.sqrt(2.0)
    max_alpha = 0.70 * math.sqrt(2.0)
    alpha = base_alpha

    s_hist = []
    y_hist = []
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    t_end = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(max_time))

    W = combined_weights(C, beta, frac)
    g = grad_from_A(A, W, L, n)

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        it += 1

        if it % 200 == 0:
            beta = min(float(beta_end), beta * 1.05)
            frac = max(float(frac_end), frac * 0.97)

        if stall > 100:
            a = spectral_phase_restart(a, L, n, S, rng, frac=0.75)
            C, A = convolve_a(a, L, n)
            cmax = float(C.max())
            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c = cmax
                best_a = a.copy()
            W = combined_weights(C, beta, frac)
            g = grad_from_A(A, W, L, n)
            s_hist = []
            y_hist = []
            alpha = base_alpha
            stall = 0
            continue

        W = combined_weights(C, beta, frac)
        g = grad_from_A(A, W, L, n)

        if len(s_hist) > 0:
            d = lbfgs_direction(s_hist, y_hist, g)
        else:
            d = -g

        d = d - d.mean()
        nd = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if nd < 1e-14:
            d = -g
            nd = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
            if nd < 1e-14:
                break
        d = d / nd

        trial_best_c = cmax
        trial_a = None
        trial_C = None
        trial_A = None

        al = alpha
        for _ in range(7):
            cand = safe_nat(a + al * d, S)
            Cc, Ac = convolve_a(cand, L, n)
            cc = float(Cc.max())
            if cc < trial_best_c - 1e-12:
                trial_best_c = cc
                trial_a = cand.copy()
                trial_C = Cc
                trial_A = Ac
            if cc <= cmax:
                break
            al *= 0.5

        if trial_a is not None and trial_best_c < cmax - 1e-12:
            Wt = combined_weights(trial_C, beta, frac)
            gt = grad_from_A(trial_A, Wt, L, n)
            s_k = trial_a - a
            y_k = gt - g
            sy = float(np.dot(s_k, y_k))
            ss = float(np.dot(s_k, s_k))
            if ss > 1e-20 and sy > 1e-14 * ss:
                s_hist.append(s_k)
                y_hist.append(y_k)
                if len(s_hist) > 10:
                    s_hist.pop(0)
                    y_hist.pop(0)

            a = trial_a
            C = trial_C
            A = trial_A
            cmax = trial_best_c
            g = gt
            alpha = min(max_alpha, alpha * 1.12)
            stall = 0

            if cmax < best_c:
                best_c = cmax
                best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            alpha = max(base_alpha * 0.25, alpha * 0.65)
            stall += 1

        if it % 2000 == 0:
            print("local cmax=%.6f  it=%d  beta=%.1f  frac=%.5f  stall=%d" %
                  (best_c, it, beta, frac, stall))

    return best_a, best_c


def search_n(n, budget, rng, parent=None):
    """Run the combined search for a fixed length n."""
    if budget <= 5.0:
        a = safe_nat(np.ones(n), math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)))
        C, _ = convolve_a(a, (1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()), n)
        return a, float(C.max())

    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    L = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()

    best_a = None
    best_c = np.inf

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget) - 40.0)

    U = make_uniform_C(L, n, S)

    # Stage 1: sign-space search from purely flat target.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0:
        ta = min(300.0, rem * 0.35)
        a_f, c_f = sqrt_target_search(U, L, n, S, ta, rng)
        if a_f is not None and c_f < best_c:
            best_a = a_f.copy()
            best_c = float(c_f)
            print("n=%d flat target cmax=%.6f" % (n, best_c))

    # Ensure we have a valid incumbent.
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = safe_nat(np.ones(n), S)
        Ctmp, _ = convolve_a(best_a, L, n)
        best_c = float(Ctmp.max())

    # Stage 2: polish the flat-target result.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0:
        tl = min(240.0, rem * 0.25)
        a_l, c_l = local_lse_block(best_a, n, S, L, tl, rng,
                                   beta_start=5.0, beta_end=80.0,
                                   frac_start=0.20, frac_end=0.010)
        if a_l is not None and c_l < best_c:
            best_a = a_l.copy()
            best_c = float(c_l)
            print("n=%d after initial local cmax=%.6f" % (n, best_c))

    # Stage 3: diverse structured starts, short local optimization.
    inits = []
    if parent is not None and len(parent) == n:
        p = np.clip(np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.sum() > 1e-9:
            inits.append(p)
    inits.append(make_init_rand(n, rng))
    inits.append(make_init_chirp(n, rng))
    inits.append(make_init_zadoff(n, rng))

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if inits and rem > 80.0:
        init_budget = min(240.0, rem * 0.15)
        per = max(5.0, init_budget / float(len(inits)))
        for arr in inits:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 40.0:
                break
            a_l, c_l = local_lse_block(arr, n, S, L, min(per, rem - 20.0), rng,
                                       beta_start=4.0, beta_end=100.0,
                                       frac_start=0.25, frac_end=0.015)
            if a_l is not None and c_l < best_c:
                best_a = a_l.copy()
                best_c = float(c_l)
                print("n=%d init local cmax=%.6f" % (n, best_c))

    # Stage 4: alternating target construction + sign search + local polishing.
    cycle = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0 and cycle < 30:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 80.0:
            break

        theta = min(0.90, 0.45 + 0.08 * cycle)
        cap_factor = max(1.0, 1.30 - 0.06 * min(cycle, 6))
        Ct = make_target_C(best_a, L, n, S, theta, cap_factor)

        local_start = best_a
        ts = min(200.0, rem * 0.45)
        if ts > 20.0:
            a_s, c_s = sqrt_target_search(Ct, L, n, S, ts, rng)
            if a_s is not None:
                if c_s < best_c:
                    best_a = a_s.copy()
                    best_c = float(c_s)
                    print("n=%d target sign cmax=%.6f" % (n, best_c))
                if c_s < max(best_c, 1e-9) * 1.08:
                    local_start = a_s

        rem2 = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem2 > 30.0 and local_start is not None:
            beta0 = 20.0 + 8.0 * min(cycle, 12)
            beta1 = 1200.0
            frac0 = max(0.02, 0.12 / (1.0 + cycle))
            a_l, c_l = local_lse_block(local_start, n, S, L, min(rem2 - 20.0, 320.0), rng,
                                       beta_start=beta0, beta_end=beta1,
                                       frac_start=frac0, frac_end=0.001)
            if a_l is not None and c_l < best_c:
                best_a = a_l.copy()
                best_c = float(c_l)
                print("n=%d cycle %d local cmax=%.6f" % (n, cycle, best_c))

        cycle += 1

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = safe_nat(np.ones(n), S)
        Ctmp, _ = convolve_a(best_a, L, n)
        best_c = float(Ctmp.max())

    return best_a, best_c


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Entry point invoked by the evaluator."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    budget = float(budget_s)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    reserve = 90.0
    search_budget = max(120.0, budget - reserve)

    n = 8192
    S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if arr.size == n:
                parent = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    try:
        best_a, best_score = search_n(n, search_budget, rng, parent)
    except Exception as e:
        print("search_n exception: %r" % (e,))
        best_a = safe_nat(np.ones(n), S)
        Ctmp, _ = convolve_a(best_a, 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length(), n)
        best_score = float(Ctmp.max())

    best_a = safe_nat(best_a, S)
    print("pre-verify internal score=%.6f len=%d" % (best_score, len(best_a)))

    final_list = [float(x) for x in best_a]
    verified = None
    try:
        verified = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        print("evaluate_sequence=%.6f" % verified)
    except Exception as e:
        print("evaluation exception: %r" % (e,))
        verified = np.nan

    if verified is None or not np.isfinite(verified):
        # Safe fallback: constant sequence is always valid and gives score 2.
        print("fallback constant sequence")
        final_list = [1.0] * 256

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("finished in %.1fs" % elapsed)
    return final_list
```
